### [1] [Vibe Coding Ate My Homework: An evaluation of AI approaches to greenfield software engineering and programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18293)
*Callum Barbour*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了"氛围编程"（vibe coding）——即使用自然语言提示而非传统代码语法进行编程的范式——在全新软件工程任务中的可行性，并分析了相关基准测试，同时开发了一套评估套件用于分析LLM在Python简单独立编程任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展正推动人机交互方式的范式转变，自然语言编程（氛围编程）有望成为高级编程方法输入的终点，消除人类对代码语法的依赖，转而使用母语进行编程。本文旨在评估这一新兴范式的可行性。

Method: 开发了一套评估套件，用于分析LLM在执行简单、独立的Python全新编程任务时的熟练程度，以提供针对性的洞察。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体结果，仅描述了研究目标和所采用的评估方法。

Conclusion: 氛围编程代表了编程领域自诞生以来所追求的方向——更高级的抽象层次，有望成为高级编程方法输入的终点。

Abstract: Thanks to rapid developments in generative AI, we are in the midst of a paradigm shift that may change how we interact with computers forever. We have observed a growth in the use of natural language prompts to build applications and coding infrastructures without underlying knowledge of the field, and this practice has been dubbed `vibe coding.' It arguably represents what the field of programming has been building towards since the beginning, with every higher level of abstraction that is conceived. Vibe coding promises to be the endpoint for the meta of high-level programming as far as method of input is concerned: eliminating a human's use of code syntax entirely in favour of programming in their mother tongue. This paper aims to evaluate the viability of vibe coding for greenfield software engineering tasks, as well as analyse the benchmarks that have been used to measure its software engineering prowess. To this end, we have developed an evaluation suite for analysing an LLM's proficiency in carrying out simple, isolated greenfield programming tasks in Python to provide scoped insight on the matter.

</details>


### [2] [Exploring Statistical Change Point Detection Techniques for Performance Anomaly Detection at Mozilla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18377)
*Mohamed Bilel Besbes,Gregory Mierzwinski,Suhaib Mujahid,Philipp Leitner,Alexander Serebrenik,Dave Hunt,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了25种变化点检测方法和15种集成方法作为Mozilla当前基于T检验的性能回归检测方法的替代方案，基于11名Mozilla工程师标注的174个性能时间序列数据集，发现集成投票策略可在F1分数上提升11%，并验证了结果的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mozilla现有的基于Student's T-test的性能异常检测方法存在12.5%的误报率和6.8%的漏报率，需要寻找更优的检测方法以降低业务影响。

Method: 构建了由11名Mozilla性能工程师手动标注的174个性能时间序列的地面真值数据集，系统评估了25种变化点检测方法和15种集成方法，并通过从业者调查验证实验结果。

Result: 离线和混合CPD方法提高了召回率但降低了精确率；集成投票策略有效缓解了精确率-召回率权衡，F1分数提升11%。

Conclusion: 集成方法比单一CPD方法更适用于性能回归检测场景，相关经验已融入Mozilla的性能工程系统。

Abstract: Software performance regressions can have significant business consequences, making automated detection a critical component of modern continuous integration pipelines. At Mozilla, performance anomaly detection is handled by Perfherder, Mozilla's performance engineering management system that relies on a Student's T-test-based approach to flag regressions across hundreds of daily code changes. However, our preliminary analysis of one year of Mozilla performance data reveals that 12.5% of generated alert groups are false positives, while approximately 6.8% of them contain regressions missed by the automated system.
  This paper presents an empirical study evaluating 25 change-point detection (CPD) methods and 15 ensemble approaches as alternatives to Mozilla's current method. We construct a ground-truth dataset of 174 performance time series manually annotated by eleven Mozilla performance engineers, representing one of the first practitioner-annotated CPD benchmarks for performance engineering. Our results show that while offline and hybrid CPD methods improve recall over Mozilla's method, they do so at a high cost to precision. Ensemble voting strategies alleviate this trade-off and offer more consistent performance, resulting in 11% improvement in the F1-score. We validate the experimental results through a practitioner survey and report on lessons learned from integrating the best methods into Mozilla's performance engineering system.

</details>


### [3] [Finding Compiler-Platform Interaction Bugs in Deep Learning Pipelines via Cross-Layer Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18421)
*Yuxin Qiu,Jiyuan Wang,Ronak Badhe,Ben Limpanukorn,Miryung Kim,Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出XCheck框架，通过提取全栈约束自动生成测试用例，用于检测深度学习编译器在跨编译层与硬件平台交互中产生的缺陷（如内存溢出、整数溢出等），并在TVM、ONNX-MLIR等编译器上发现2034个缺陷案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DL编译器测试方法主要关注类型约束和输入生成限制，侧重于类型验证、编译崩溃检测或覆盖率提升，但忽略了编译器与硬件平台交互过程中因跨层效应引发的缺陷。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) 自动化提取全栈约束，联合指导模型生成并表征编译行为；(2) 优先选择暴露交互敏感行为的约束，使生成模型能执行深层编译逻辑；(3) 通过自动插入断言监控覆盖率或通过/失败信号无法捕捉的差异化编译症状，实现行为等价划分。

Result: 在TVM、ONNX-MLIR等三个主流DL编译器上评估，发现2034个缺陷案例，包括内存溢出、整数溢出以及根源于编译器-平台交互的静默异常编译行为。

Conclusion: 全栈约束驱动的测试方法比仅约束输入生成更有效，能系统性地发现跨编译层与硬件平台交互导致的缺陷，提升DL编译器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The growing deployment of artificial intelligence (AI) necessitates robust deep learning (DL) compilers, such as TVM and ONNX-MLIR. These compilers take as input high-level AI models, lower them through multi-layer transformations, and specialize them to diverse hardware. Testing such compilers is uniquely challenging as correctness depends on implicit constraints embedded throughout the compilation stack. Existing testing approaches largely take type constraints to restrict input model generation and therefore emphasize type validation and monitor compilation crashes or coverage gains. This focus overlooks compiler-platform interaction bugs that arise from interleaved effects across compilation and execution environments.
  In this work, we propose a scalable, automated DL compiler testing framework for, in tandem, (1) finding compiler-platform interaction bugs and (2) enabling behavior equivalence partitioning. Our key insight is that these bugs are caused by violated assumptions arising from interactions across compilation passes and hardware platforms. Therefore, we move beyond constraining input generation and derive full-stack constraints. Our approach is three-fold. First, we design an automated approach to extract full-stack constraints that jointly guide model generation and characterize compilation behaviors. Second, we prioritize constraints that expose interaction-sensitive behaviors, so our generated models are capable of exercising deep compilation logic. Third, we enable behavior equivalence partitioning by automatically inserting assertions to monitor distinct compilation symptoms that coverage or pass/fail signals miss.
  We evaluated our tool, XCheck, on three widely-used DL compilers and found 2,034 bug-revealing cases, including memory overflows, integer overflows, and silent unexpected compilations that were rooted in compiler-platform interactions.

</details>


### [4] [A Critical Discourse Analysis of Gender Representation in Software Engineering Education Videos on YouTube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18423)
*Isabella Graßl,Alexander Serebrenik,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对YouTube上200个英德软件工程教程进行批判性话语分析，发现男性角色和男性语言占主导地位，存在“能动性差距”：技术决策角色几乎全分配给男性，女性角色被动或缺失，表明YouTube上的软件工程教育可能通过语言和表征上的把关再生产性别不平等，形成象征性障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育资源可能影响学生对软件工程领域归属感的认知，而该领域长期存在性别差距。然而，在线学习空间中关于性别的隐性课程鲜为人知，因此需要探究YouTube教程中的性别表征问题。

Method: 对200个YouTube上的英语和德语软件工程教程进行手动批判性话语分析，通过语境领域和语言身份标记考察性别表征。

Result: 男性角色和男性化语言默认值在教程中占主导地位；发现存在“能动性差距”，即技术和决策角色几乎全由男性承担，女性角色要么缺席要么倾向于被动、低能动性的角色。

Conclusion: YouTube上的软件工程教育可能再生产性别规范，其中语言和表征上的把关可能成为软件工程领域的象征性壁垒。

Abstract: Educational resources may frame students' perceptions of who belongs in software engineering, which is relevant given the field's ongoing gender gap. However, we know little about the hidden curriculum regarding gender in online learning spaces. This study presents a critical discourse analysis of 200 manually analysed English and German software engineering tutorials on YouTube, examining gender representation through contextual domains and linguistic identity markers. Our results show that male characters and masculine linguistic defaults dominate the tutorials. We identified an agency gap, in which technical and decision-making roles are almost exclusively assigned to male actors, while female actors are either absent or tend to passive, low-agency roles. The findings indicate that software engineering education on YouTube may reproduce gendered norms, in which linguistic and representational gatekeeping may serve as a symbolic barrier to software engineering.

</details>


### [5] [From Specification to Execution: AI Assisted Scientific Workflow Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18425)
*Komal Thareja,Hamza Safri,Rajiv Mayani,Anirban Mandal,Ewa Deelman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合规范驱动的工作流生成、自动化调试和分布式执行的AI辅助科学工作流管理方法，通过结构化规范阶段分离工作流意图、设计和实现，并集成Pegasus与MCP层支持分布式执行。在医疗影像联邦学习工作流上的评估表明，该方法能生成和执行包含数千个作业的大规模工作流，减少调试工作量，并使非专家用户能够使用专家级设计模式构建工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学工作流管理系统中，工作流的设计、实现和调试仍然主要依赖人工，需要大量专业知识。现有基于LLM的工作流生成方法多依赖直接代码合成，透明性、可重复性和与工作流系统的集成性受限。

Method: 1) 引入结构化规范阶段，将工作流意图、设计和实现分离，在代码生成前进行验证；2) 开发基于LLM的调试代理，诊断和解决跨多个系统层的故障；3) 集成Pegasus WMS与MCP层，提供统一的工作流提交、监控和控制接口。

Result: 在医疗影像联邦学习工作流上评估，系统成功生成并执行包含数千个作业的大规模工作流，显著减少了调试工作量，非专家用户能够构建出达到专家设计模式水平的工作流。

Conclusion: 端到端AI辅助的工作流生成和执行是可行的，该工作指向了AI驱动平台管理科学工作流全生命周期的未来方向。

Abstract: Scientific workflow management systems (WMS) support scalable and reproducible execution of complex pipelines, but workflow design, implementation, and debugging remain largely manual and require significant expertise. Recent approaches using large language models (LLMs) show promise for workflow generation from natural language, but often rely on direct code synthesis, which limits transparency, reproducibility, and integration with workflow systems. We present an AI-assisted approach to scientific workflow management that combines specification-driven workflow generation, automated debugging, and distributed execution. The method introduces a structured specification phase that separates workflow intent, design, and implementation, allowing validation prior to code generation. We also develop an LLM-based debugging agent that diagnoses and resolves failures across multiple system layers. To support distributed execution and user interaction, we integrate Pegasus, a widely used WMS, with a Model Context Protocol (MCP) layer, providing a unified interface for workflow submission, monitoring, and control. We evaluate the approach using a federated learning workflow for medical imaging, chosen for its parallel, iterative, and dependency-intensive structure. The system generated and executed large-scale workflows with thousands of jobs, reduced debugging effort, and allowed non-expert users to construct workflows with expert-level design patterns. These results indicate that end-to-end AI-assisted workflow generation and execution is feasible, and point toward AI-driven platforms for managing the scientific workflow lifecycle.

</details>


### [6] [Designing L5: A Permacomputing Approach to Creative Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18481)
*Lee Tusman,Kit Kuksenok*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了L5——一个基于LOVE框架、使用Lua实现的Processing/p5家族新成员。L5将永续计算原则（受永续农业启发的可持续计算理念）引入创意编码领域，并通过五个案例研究探讨了可持续性与可用性之间的设计张力：平衡感知简洁性与暴露底层机制、降低资源消耗设计、确保长期稳定性、约束功能范围、以及为资源受限环境设计文档。研究认为，可持续创意工具需要在竞争性价值之间进行透明协商，而非优化单一指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意编码库（如Processing/p5）为艺术家和学习者提供了易于使用的计算与算法艺术工具，但其设计理念较少关注可持续性。永续计算作为一种新兴运动，致力于解决计算领域的可持续性问题，但其价值观尚未被创意编码社区广泛接纳。本研究旨在探索如何将永续计算原则融入创意编码工具设计中，填补这一空白。

Method: 通过五个案例研究来分析L5的设计决策：1）平衡感知简洁性与暴露底层实现细节；2）为降低资源消耗进行设计；3）确保长期稳定性；4）约束功能范围以限制资源使用；5）为资源受限环境下访问文档进行设计。这些案例系统地审视了可持续性与可用性之间的张力。

Result: 研究揭示了可持续性与可用性之间存在多种竞争性价值，且无法通过单一指标优化来简单解决。每个设计决策都涉及权衡，例如在简化用户界面与暴露底层机制之间的取舍、在丰富功能与限制资源消耗之间的平衡等。

Conclusion: 可持续创意工具的设计不是追求单一指标的优化，而是需要在不同价值之间进行透明的协商与权衡。这为未来创意编码工具在可持续性方面的设计实践提供了新的思路和框架。

Abstract: Creative coding libraries provide high-level tools that make computational and algorithmic art accessible to artists and learners. Processing/p5 is one such family of libraries, known for its beginner-friendly approach and wide reach across artistic and technical communities. L5 is a new member of this family, implemented in Lua using the LOVE framework. It applies permacomputing principles, a movement addressing sustainability in computing inspired by permaculture, bringing these values to a community of practice not historically centered on them. This paper explores L5's design decisions and tensions between sustainability and usability through five case studies: 1. balancing perceived simplicity versus exposing the seams, 2. designing for lower resource consumption, 3. ensuring long-term stability, 4. constraining functionality, and 5. designing documentation for resource-constrained access. Rather than optimizing for a single metric, sustainable creative tools require navigating competing values transparently.

</details>


### [7] [SWE-Future: Forecast-Conditioned Data Synthesis for Future-Oriented Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18733)
*Qiao Zhao,JianYing Qu,Jun Zhang,Yehua Yang,Hanwen Du,Zhongkai Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Future提出了一种基于预测条件的数据合成方法，利用代码仓库在时间T0前的证据预测未来的功能实现、增强、缺陷修复和重构任务，并通过80个仓库的回顾性验证（未来工作相关性达58.1%）后，合成200个编码智能体任务，减少对历史PR回放的直接依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准测试存在两个问题：(1) 基于真实GitHub issue/PR的基准容易与模型预训练、微调、合成数据生成或基准驱动模型选择产生数据重叠；(2) 完全合成任务虽然避免了历史回放，但可能偏离真实仓库需求。因此需要一种既能避免数据泄露又能保持与现实仓库需求一致的任务合成方法。

Method: 提出SWE-Future方法：在时间T0给定预测快照，仅使用T0之前的仓库证据来预测未来的功能实现/增强、缺陷修复和重构任务家族。首先通过回顾性验证——固定预测结果后，使用后续PR仅衡量预测任务家族是否匹配未来仓库工作；然后使用验证有效的预测家族作为条件信号，从任务生成快照中合成200个编码智能体数据集，而非回放用于验证的未来PR。

Result: 在80个仓库的回顾性研究中，预测器在主语义匹配指标上达到58.1%的未来工作相关性。基于验证后的预测家族，从61个仓库的任务生成快照合成了200个编码智能体任务数据集。

Conclusion: SWE-Future证明了仓库演化预测可以指导逼真的、面向未来的编码任务合成，同时减少对历史PR回放的直接依赖，为编码智能体基准测试提供了一种兼顾真实性与数据隔离的新范式。

Abstract: Realistic coding-agent benchmarks often replay public GitHub issues and pull requests, making them vulnerable to overlap with model pretraining, fine-tuning, synthetic-data generation, or benchmark-driven model selection. Fully synthetic tasks avoid direct historical replay, but can drift away from real repository needs. We propose SWE-Future, a forecast-conditioned data synthesis method for future-oriented coding tasks. Given a forecast snapshot at time $T_0$, the method uses only pre-$T_0$ repository evidence to forecast future feature implementation/enhancement, bugfix, and refactor task families. We first validate this forecasting step retrospectively: after forecasts are fixed, later pull requests are used only to measure whether the predicted task families match future repository work. In an 80-repository study, the forecaster achieves 58.1\% future-work relevance under the main semantic matching metric. We then use validated forecast families as conditioning signals to synthesize a 200-task coding-agent dataset across 61 repositories from a task-generation snapshot, rather than replaying the later pull requests used for validation. SWE-Future shows that repository-evolution forecasts can guide realistic, future-oriented coding-task synthesis while reducing direct dependence on historical pull-request replay.

</details>


### [8] [Toward Semantically-Seeded, Graph-Propagated Impact Analysis Across Software Artifacts: A Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18855)
*Momil Seedat*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、可解释的融合分析方法，结合语义相似度（文本嵌入）和结构依赖（静态分析传播）进行变更影响分析，弥补单一方法的盲区，并通过混合参数λ控制精度/召回率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变更影响分析工具仅依赖语义相似度或结构依赖中的单一信号，各自存在盲区：语义方法无法发现无词汇重叠的受影响工件，结构方法无法捕获无直接依赖但有语义关联的工件，且多数工具仅覆盖代码而非需求-配置-服务-测试全链路。

Method: 将系统建模为异构工件图（通过静态分析恢复类型化边），计算变更工件与所有工件的余弦相似度作为语义先验，通过行归一化的传播矩阵进行多跳衰减传播，将语义与结构分数通过可调参数λ进行融合。

Result: 在支付子系统的5个标注变更场景上的概念验证表明：融合方法能同时覆盖两种盲区——通过传播恢复零文本重叠的受影响工件，通过语义层恢复纯传播无法到达的辅助函数，λ可作为显式的精度/召回率控制旋钮。

Conclusion: 融合信号的方法优于单一信号方法，且该框架可扩展到运营工件（镜像、指标、仪表盘、数据模式），弥补代码级分析的不足。

Abstract: When a single software artifact changes - a requirement, a configuration value, or a function - engineers must determine what else is impacted. Existing change-impact-analysis (CIA) tooling tends to rely on one of two signals in isolation: semantic similarity recovered from text (information-retrieval traceability, code search, embeddings), or structural dependency following (call graphs, IDE "find usages", test-impact selection). Each has a characteristic blind spot. A semantically driven tool misses an impacted artifact whose text shares no vocabulary with the change; a structurally driven tool misses artifacts related in meaning but not joined by an edge, and most operate only over code rather than the Requirement-Config-Service-Test chain. We argue for a training-free and interpretable analyzer that fuses both signals over the same embeddings. We model the system as a heterogeneous artifact graph with typed edges recovered by static analysis, compute a semantic prior by cosine similarity to the changed artifact, propagate impact multi-hop with decay over a row-normalized propagation matrix, and blend the two with a single tunable weight lambda. A small but complete proof-of-concept on a payment subsystem (5 labelled change scenarios) shows the mechanism we care about: artifacts with zero textual overlap with the change are still recovered through propagation, and helper functions that propagation alone cannot reach are recovered through the semantic layer. The fusion is the only configuration that covers both blind spots, and lambda acts as an explicit precision/recall control. Drawing on four publicly documented production failures, we argue that the same formulation extends to operational artifacts (images, metrics, dashboards, data schemas) that code-only analysis cannot reach.

</details>


### [9] [CAPRA: Scaling Feedback on Software Architecture Deliverables with a Multi-Agent LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18976)
*Marco Becattini,Niccolò Caselli,Matteo Minin,Roberto Verdecchia,Enrico Vicario*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CAPRA是一个多智能体LLM系统，用于自动化评估软件架构交付物，通过确定性证据锚定和一致性管理器来缓解幻觉问题，在10份学生报告上达到88.8%的评价标准满足率，每份报告处理时间略超4分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程教育中代码评分和论文评分已实现自动化，但软件架构交付物的评估——需要分析结构完整性和需求可追溯性——尚未完全自动化。

Method: CAPRA采用多智能体LLM架构，包含：1) Python微服务结合PyMuPDF和视觉LLM(gpt-4o)进行多模态文档提取；2) 基于归一化Levenshtein距离的确定性证据锚定步骤；3) ConsistencyManager智能体进行交叉验证、去重和合并；4) 生成个性化、模板合规的LaTeX反馈。

Result: 在10份学生报告上，CAPRA在严格的两评估者聚合规则下满足88.8%的评价标准，与人类评估者达到中等一致性(kappa=0.582)，每份报告处理时间略超4分钟。

Conclusion: LLM支持的架构反馈具有可行性，但主观评估维度仍需人类监督。

Abstract: Automated assessment in software engineering education has advanced significantly for code grading and essay scoring. However, reviewing software architecture deliverables, which requires analyzing structural completeness and requirements traceability, has not yet been fully automated. Applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to this task requires robust architectures to ensure technical feedback is accurate and reliable for students. This paper presents CAPRA (Configurable Architecture Proficiency Report Assessment), a multi-agent LLM system that analyzes software architecture deliverables to generate personalized, template-compliant LaTeX feedback. As a core design choice, CAPRA coordinates multiple specialized agents and employs a Python-based microservice for multi-modal document extraction, utilizing PyMuPDF and vision-enabled LLMs (specifically gpt-4o) to parse text and UML diagrams. To ensure educational reliability and mitigate hallucinations, CAPRA introduces a deterministic Evidence Anchoring step using fuzzy matching via normalized Levenshtein distance, along with a ConsistencyManager agent that cross-verifies, deduplicates, and merges findings. System performance is assessed using a structured eight-criterion binary evaluation taxonomy covering: (i) extraction completeness, (ii) feature validation, (iii) issue grounding and severity detection, (iv) recommendation specificity and traceability, and (v) template and tone compliance. A preliminary empirical evaluation on 10 student reports shows that CAPRA satisfied 88.8% of the evaluated criteria under a strict two-rater aggregation rule, achieved moderate inter-rater agreement with human evaluators (kappa = 0.582), and processed each report in slightly over 4 minutes. While these results support the viability of LLM-supported architectural feedback, human oversight remains essential for subjective assessment dimensions.

</details>


### [10] [Where Did the Variability Go? From Vibe Coding to Product Lines by Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19042)
*Xhevahire Tërnava*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索了AI驱动编程范式"vibe coding"中变异性管理的挑战，发现AI生成的代码在编译和运行时几乎没有变异性（所有变异性在生成时即被解决），并提出了"变异性通过再生"（VbR）方法——将LLM作为派生引擎，根据声明式规范为每个变体生成无死代码的专用二进制文件，再通过变体分发器透明路由用户请求。该方法在wc产品家族上得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统的软件工程中，变异性（如条件编译、配置选项等）被精心构建到代码中以支持不同场景。然而在"vibe coding"这一新兴AI驱动编程范式下，LLM从自然语言提示生成完整程序，这种变异性如何处理？作者旨在探索AI生成代码中的变异性状态并提出解决方案。

Method: 首先对10个vibe coded的C/C++项目进行探索性分析，发现编译时和运行时几乎没有变异性（near-zero in-artifact variability）。然后提出变异性通过再生（VbR）方法：将LLM作为派生引擎，从声明式规范出发，为每个变体独立生成无死代码的专用二进制，并通过变体分发器透明地将用户请求路由到匹配的二进制。对VbR进行了形式化描述，与经典SPL派生进行了对比，并在wc产品家族上演示了完整流程。

Result: 分析的10个vibe coded项目显示AI生成代码中的变异性接近于零，所有变异性决策在生成时（generation time）即被解决。提出的VbR方法成功地在wc产品家族上实现了从声明式规范生成多种变体二进制，并实现了透明路由。VbR避免了传统SPL中死代码和配置复杂性问题。

Conclusion: 对于软件产品线工程，AI生成软件中的变异性应当属于规范（specification），而非代码本身。VbR提供了一种新的产品线方法，利用LLM作为派生引擎，克服了传统SPL在AI生成代码场景下的局限性。

Abstract: In vibe coding, an emerging AI-driven paradigm, an LLM generates an entire program from a natural language prompt, but what happens to the variability that traditional software engineering carefully builds into code? To answer this question, we conducted an exploratory analysis on 10 vibe coded C/C++ projects, which suggests that there is near-zero in-artifact variability, i.e., at compile and runtime. All variability decisions are resolved at a single new binding time, generation time, the moment the LLM produces the source code. Rather than treating this as a defect to fix, we propose Variability by Regeneration (VbR), to our knowledge the first product-line approach in which the LLM acts as the derivation engine, generating a purpose-built, free of dead code binary for each variant from a declarative specification, while a variant dispatcher transparently routes user requests to the matching binary. We formalise VbR, contrast it with classical SPL derivation, and demonstrate its full pipeline on a wc product family. For SPL engineering, variability in AI-generated software belongs in the specification, not in the code.

</details>


### [11] [Written by AI, Managed by AI: Semantic Space Control and Index Sickness Elimination Across 391 Consecutive Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19121)
*Hui Zhang,Shuren Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项长达一个月、391次协作会话的真实软件工程项目，揭示了在长周期LLM协作中，增加符号化约束（如标识符系统、防御规则、扩展上下文）反而会导致模型放弃理解业务语义，陷入自我指涉推理并产生脱离现实的输出。作者将这种失败模式命名为“索引病”（Index Sickness），其典型表现为“幽灵立法”（Phantom Legislation），并提出了“庞氏原理（语义活力法则）”——承载明确目的的自然语言信息质量远高于符号表达。基于此原理设计了“基线-日志物理分离”机制，在实践中将AI指令量减少约75%，并在后续约150次会话中未再出现这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有工程直觉——即通过增加形式化约束（符号系统、防御规则、大上下文窗口）来应对长周期LLM协作中的概念漂移问题，并揭示该策略在实际应用中可能适得其反。

Method: 行动研究方法（Action Research），在真实软件项目Bang-v3中开展为期约一个月、涵盖391次协作会话的实证研究，记录并分析符号化约束策略的失败过程。

Result: 符号系统超过复杂度阈值后，LLM不再提升准确性，而是放弃对业务语义的理解，退入符号层的自我指涉推理，产生内部一致但与现实脱节的输出。基于“基线-日志物理分离”机制，AI指令量减少约75%，后续约150次会话中未再出现索引病。

Conclusion: 自然语言的语义活力优于符号化约束，物理分离基线逻辑与运行日志是解决长周期LLM协作中概念漂移的有效工程机制。

Abstract: The prevailing engineering intuition for addressing conceptual drift in long-horizon LLM collaboration is to trade more formal constraints for more reliable outputs -- designing symbolic identifier systems, accumulating defensive rules in System Prompts, expanding context windows. Our engineering record shows that in long-horizon settings, this direction may produce effects contrary to design intent. Using action research methods in a real software project (Bang-v3) spanning approximately one month and 391 collaborative sessions, we document and analyze the failure process of these strategies. When the symbolic system exceeds a complexity threshold, LLMs do not become more accurate -- instead, they abandon genuine understanding of business semantics, retreat to self-referential reasoning within the symbolic layer, and generate outputs that appear internally consistent but are physically disconnected from reality. We name this failure pattern "Index Sickness," and its canonical manifestation "Phantom Legislation." We name the underlying principle the "Pang Principle (Semantic Vitality Law)": natural language carrying explicit purpose conveys far greater information quality than symbolic expression. From this, we design and validate its physical engineering mechanism: "Baseline-Log Physical Separation." In the same project, this mechanism reduced AI Instructions volume by ~75%, and across the subsequent ~150 sessions, no recurrence of Index Sickness was observed. A bilingual companion version (Chinese) is included as supplementary material.

</details>


### [12] [Teaching Software Engineering with LLM and MCP Integration: From Classroom to Industry Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19167)
*Kehui Chen,Jacky Keung,Weining Li,Xiangbing Shao,Yishu Li,Xiaoxue Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种将LLM和MCP集成到软件工程协作教学模型中的创新方法，通过嵌入智能工具到日常教学、代码辅助和工程模拟中，结合行业实习，弥合传统教学与工业实践之间的差距，提升学生编程能力、问题解决能力和智能工具使用能力，为AI时代的软件工程教育改革提供了实践路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和MCP在工业软件工程中的快速集成，使得软件工程教育亟需更新以跟上新兴技术和不断变化的行业需求。

Method: 将LLM和MCP驱动的工具嵌入日常教学、代码辅助和工程模拟中，构建与真实工程实践紧密相连的实用学习框架，并通过行业实习合作伙伴关系让学生在实际环境中应用这些技术。

Result: 该模型有效弥合了传统教学与工业工作流之间的差距，提升了学生的编程能力、实际问题解决能力和智能工程工具的使用熟练度。

Conclusion: 本研究为人工智能时代软件工程教育的改革与创新提供了一条实践路径。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) into industrial software engineering has created a pressing need to update software engineering education to align with emerging technologies and evolving industry demands. This study investigates an innovative approach that integrates LLMs and MCP into a collaborative teaching model for software engineering education, aiming to build a practical learning framework closely connected to real-world engineering practices. By embedding LLM and MCP driven tools into daily teaching, code assistance, and engineering simulations, the model effectively bridges the gap between traditional instruction and industrial workflows. This integration enhances students' programming competence, practical problem-solving abilities, and proficiency in using intelligent engineering tools. Furthermore, through partnerships with industry internships, students can apply these technologies in real-world settings, further strengthening the connection between academic preparation and professional practice. Overall, this research offers a practical pathway for reforming and innovating software engineering education in the era of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [13] [No Two Developers Think Alike: How Problem-Solving Styles and Experience Shape Needs in Conversational Interaction with Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19216)
*Jonan Richards,Bruno Alves de Oliveira,Iury Oliveira,Igor Wiese,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过混合方法出声思考研究（27名专业开发者和学生），本文探索了认知多样性如何影响开发者与GitHub Copilot Chat的交互，识别出5种不同的"交互模式"和10种潜在需求，并建立了概念模型，揭示了交互模式、需求与开发者问题解决风格及经验之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示不同开发者对编程助手的需求存在差异，且特定开发者群体会遇到独特的挑战，但缺乏对认知多样性如何塑造这些交互的系统性理解。

Method: 采用混合方法的出声思考研究，对27名专业开发者和学生进行实验，分析其与GitHub Copilot Chat的交互过程，识别交互模式和潜在需求，并关联开发者的问题解决风格和经验特征。

Result: 识别出5种不同的交互模式和10种潜在需求，构建了概念模型；发现这些模式和需求与开发者的问题解决风格和经验水平之间存在关联。

Conclusion: 认知多样性会显著影响开发者与编程助手的交互方式，建议研究者和从业者在设计、研究和应用编程助手时，应更好地考虑不同开发者的多样化需求。

Abstract: Conversational LLM-based ``programming assistants'' provide a range of benefits to developers. However, recent studies demonstrate the variety in individual developers' needs regarding programming assistants, and challenges encountered by only specific groups of developers. In this study, we explore the role of cognitive diversity in shaping interactions with GitHub Copilot chat. Through a mixed-methods think aloud study with 27 professional developers and students, we characterize 5 distinct ``interaction modes'' and 10 underlying needs in developers' interactions, forming a conceptual model. We characterize links between these modes, needs, and developers' problem-solving styles and experience profiles, showing how cognitive diversity may shape developers' interactions. We provide insights and recommendations for researchers and practitioners on how to design, research, and employ programming assistants to better account for diverse developer needs.

</details>


### [14] [Runtime Compliance Verification for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19242)
*Nafiseh Kahani,Masoud Barati,Diana Addae*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出C-Trace框架，通过运行时监控在AI代理执行过程中强制执行GDPR合规要求，在模拟噪声环境下将攻击成功率控制在12%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理测试主要依赖离线红队测试或静态提示审查，无法在运行时保证代理行为符合GDPR等监管规则，存在数据合规风险。

Method: 将GDPR要求形式化为策略谓词，通过运行时监控器拦截每次工具调用和模型输出，拒绝不合规行为，并使用攻击对话（含DSPy生成提示和红队语料）进行测试。

Result: 在10%的抽取器噪声下，攻击成功率≤12%，假阳性率≤16%；在完美抽取条件下ASR降至0%。

Conclusion: C-Trace能够有效在运行时强制AI代理遵守GDPR合规规则，优于基线方法。

Abstract: AI agents now handle personal data through tool use, function calls, and multi turn dialogue, which can create obligations under the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR). Current testing practices mainly rely on offline red teaming or static prompt review, but they do not guarantee at runtime that agent behavior follows regulatory rules. We propose C-Trace (Compliance Trace based Runtime Agent Conformance Enforcement), a verification framework that: (i) expresses a subset of GDPR requirements, including consent, purpose limitation, data minimization, and the right to erasure, as formal policy predicates over agent execution traces; (ii) uses a runtime monitor that intercepts every tool invocation and model output and rejects non-compliant actions; and (iii) tests the agent with attack dialogues, including DSPy generated prompts and verbatim prompts from red teaming corpora, that try to induce violations. We evaluate the framework on four case studies reframed to GDPR. Under 10 percent per-category extractor noise, including drop-out and over-typing, the monitor keeps the attack success rate at less than or equal to 12 percent, below the baselines we compare against, and false positives at less than or equal to 16 percent, and reaches 0 percent ASR under perfect extraction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>
